Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the nonequilibrium space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 909 and the convergence rate is O(417/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the nonequilibrium space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 909 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 417. QED.
